# CORONACH decisions

- 2026-09-20 D001: Follow SLICE_PROMPT and generated-art policy where older Frostbound skill or ASSET_SOURCES suggestions disagree. No Frostbound visual art is copied, except permitted TMP/font resources, input glyphs, particles and sound.
- 2026-09-20 D002: Port the proven DirectInput bridge before gameplay. Logitech Precision's d-pad is its movement device; cycling targets/items must use a modifier on a digital-only controller so moving never changes targets/items unexpectedly.
- 2026-09-20 D003: Keep copied code/tools traceable; adapt namespaces and strip Frostbound campaign dependencies. Frostbound remains read-only.
- 2026-09-20 D004: Gate evidence stays fail-closed. Compilation, state assertions, test XML and visual inspection are separate requirements. A marker alone cannot substitute for gameplay or a render.
- 2026-09-20 D005: Pin Splines 2.9.1 ([Unity registry](https://packages.unity.com/com.unity.splines) reports minimum 2022.3) and Timeline 1.8.12 (Frostbound's working pin). Keep the requested Unity/URP versions. Shader Graph 17.4 has three unqualified GUID references that fail on 6000.4.7; reproduce Frostbound's working UnityEngine.GUID qualification through `tools/patch_unity_packages.py`, applied to this project's cache before each batch run.
- 2026-09-20 D006: Generate title key art during P0 so the title screenshot is useful. It depicts Cinder Halo and Vorun, with no unapproved species depiction. Meshy ledger uses the bridge's append-only JSON array format; the 1,200-credit cap is tracked separately and will be enforced before paid requests.
- 2026-09-20 D007: On the Logitech Precision, hold LB plus d-pad to cycle targets/items; ordinary d-pad motion never cycles. Camera yaw rotates movement into screen directions. XInput keeps its independent d-pad cycling.
- 2026-09-20 D008: Equipment output applies to the weapon being used; armour, drives, modules and affixes affect both modes. A T2 edge adds 25 to level-one Output 50, targeting a one-third reduction in fight time. Upgrades add 8% per level, capped at +5, and cost 3 x next upgrade level Scrap Alloy.
- 2026-09-20 D009: Generated UI atlases are sliced using inspected rectangles; ordinary Unity tint states provide focus feedback from the same generated button. No downloaded icon/panel art is used.
- 2026-09-20 D010: Runtime definitions live under Resources/Definitions so code-built arenas and zones can resolve stable IDs. Yarn sources/project live under Resources/Dialogue for the same reason. Assembly dependency direction is preserved.
- 2026-09-20 D011: P2 will not be marked closed merely because automated tests pass. The physical attachment log, generated screenshots, and human pad-feel requirement are recorded separately; an unattended smoke is never described as hands-on play.
- 2026-09-20 D012: Build all five zones as capsule/cube blockouts while P1 is pending. This advances route logic without spending Meshy credits or treating stand-ins as final art. Prefab names and definitions provide replacement points for generated models.
- 2026-09-20 D013: Use an open upper shell for the Gullet's spline tube so the fixed three-quarter camera can see the party. The shell faces inward; collidable side rails preserve wall-contact damage.
- 2026-09-20 D014: Tallow Drift currently arrives directly on its safe docking deck. The separate exterior approach remains part of final world dressing; the NPC, repair and completion save already work.
- 2026-09-20 D015: Record native image-tool dimensions honestly. Do not label the returned 1774x887 panoramas as 4K. Real-ESRGAN is used for the explicitly requested portrait upscale; original generated sources and prompts remain archived.
- 2026-09-20 D016: Keep a permanent material collection tally so accepting Mira's quest after mining or crafting does not make its six finite nodes impossible to complete. The quest does not consume crystals.
- 2026-09-20 D017: Give pause priority over combat slow motion. On analog pads, LB lock-on preserves stick movement and the independent d-pad only cycles. Precision retains LB+d-pad cycling.
- 2026-09-20 D018: First route smoke awarded 1,936 XP and reached level 7. Raise the XP requirement from 80 x current level to 110 x current level, targeting level 6 on that route while allowing optional clearing to reward extra levels. Instrument boss engagement duration; tune from real damage rather than log-only kill markers.
- 2026-09-20 D019: Retain an isolated development route driver and performance probe. Route uses actual gameplay actions; performance alone makes its actor invulnerable to sustain a thirty-second twelve-enemy load. These flags are excluded from release gameplay.
- 2026-09-20 D020: Combat action windows use a clock that stops during menus. Flash Move retains its real-time advantage while pause freezes invulnerability and action timers. Dialogue advancement latches a short press until the presenter consumes it, so punctuation delays cannot lose A taps.
- 2026-09-20 D021: The route driver's travel timeout excludes nested combat, which has its own 150-second deadline. The previous 75-second travel deadline incorrectly rejected a completed 97.8-second Cantor fight; the overall route timeout still bounds the run.
- 2026-09-20 D022: Raise blockout Burrower Integrity from 12,000 to 30,000 and Cantor from 18,000 to 22,000 after measured 21.0/97.8-second scripted fights. This is an initial duration correction; new measurements, the T2 comparison, generated hitbox review and physical pad tuning remain required.
- 2026-09-20 D023: Suspend party-to-save synchronization during scene loading. Retry replaces GameState before the fade unloads the defeated party; those old actors must never overwrite the restored health or active-member selection. An end-to-end regression failed on the old behavior before applying the fix.
- 2026-09-20 D024: Reject the 30,000-Integrity Burrower experiment after its 150-second timeout and repeated partner revives. Use 18,000 for the next checkpoint measurement. Preserve the failed log and add party/enemy health, positions and actor state to route failures. Duration and T2 targets remain open until measured.
- 2026-09-20 D025: Raise the route driver's in-range aim nudge from 0.08 to 0.15, above both motors' facing threshold. This corrects the instrument without changing player controls. The next Burrower sample was 64.7 seconds at 18,000 Integrity; retain that value pending physical tuning and the T2 comparison.
- 2026-09-20 D026: Complete the designed enemy attack distinctions on blockouts: pack hunters and Darts face their chosen direction during the wind-up and commit to a short lunge/dive; each attack shares one victim set. Drifters fire a three-projectile spread. Add a runtime regression for actual movement and projectile creation before treating these behaviors as verified.
- 2026-09-20 D027 — NATHAN_SPECIES_APPROVED: Nathan replied, "in general the designs of the race are fine. when we actually design the main characters they'll need to be more unique looking faces but that is okay for now. please continue". Proceed with production, retaining the species and hero silhouettes/palettes. Give Taren and Sela distinct facial proportions and markings in their production references and portraits; this is an art requirement, not another approval gate. Receipt: `docs/art/species-approval.json`. Meshy remains capped at 1,200 credits.
- 2026-09-20 D028: Taren gets a broad blunt jaw, hooded amber eyes and small nose scar; Sela gets a longer angular face, raised brow, crooked smile and three cheek speckles. Preserve these identities across forms and portraits. Early reconstruction references with hidden fingers, external ears or translucent exports were corrected with the image tool; accepted sources are indexed in `design-catalog.json`.
- 2026-09-20 D029: P1 environment boards are design documents, not Meshy inputs. Generate isolated views before reconstructing each object. Cantor is reconstructed as separate head/body/tail pieces. Run Taren Natural alone through T2 generation, humanoid rigging, animation retargeting and Unity renders before batching other paid models, as required by the inherited character skill.
- 2026-09-20 D030: The T2 pilot passes at 20 credits. Pre-create materials before model import; perform initial humanoid import before applying the explicit bone map; measure skinned vertices through bone matrices. Review poses must advance editor frames before capture because Unity caches GPU skinning within a frame. Added local joint-rotation checks so root-only motion cannot pass. Proceed with the remaining hero batch.
- 2026-09-20 D031: Portrait sheets preserve the refined hero facial identities across all sixteen expressions. Each sheet is one image-tool generation, then visually mapped, color-keyed, actually upscaled 4x with Real-ESRGAN and assembled at 2304x2304. Import the complete eight-sheet batch together.
- 2026-09-21 D032: Separate and duplicate each Shaped model's own generated vane geometry into four UV-preserving `Vane_*` parts. Attach upper parts to Chest and lower parts to Hips. Choose outward hinge directions from actual geometry so mirrored source axes cannot fold the vanes inward. Opened folded, side, Flight and overhead Flight renders; four parts now remain distinct.
- 2026-09-21 D033: `P3_PORTRAITS_OK` closes the portrait sub-checkpoint: all eight sheets imported with full 576-pixel cells, and all sixteen Neutral/Shocked dialogue captures opened in four evidence contact sheets. This does not close the complete P3 art gate.
- 2026-09-21 D034: Fox donor staging preserves the `AnimalArmature` hierarchy and removes object-level motion curves while retaining bone animation. The first export failed the frozen-walk gate; its replacement passes skin travel and visibly alternates strides. Only skeleton/clips and the CC0 licence are staged.
- 2026-09-21 D035: Preserve two shared civilian rigs and apply four Meshy retextures with original UVs. Rejected prompts over Meshy's 800-character limit are not retried unchanged. Use compact palette-specific prompts; keep the full design references and record actual credits.
- 2026-09-21 D036: The Sentinel's generated geometry is sound but its first Meshy rig has neck/head joints below the hips. Repair locally using the proven generated humanoid skeleton and new weights; visual acceptance is still required. Replacement FBXs must clear Unity's serialized old humanoid rest pose. Cantor head/body/tail receive explicit inspected axis corrections before chain review.
- 2026-09-21 D037: Generate every environment prop as its own white-background reconstruction image. Replace the busy Gullet membrane with a quieter generated navy/violet material, preserving the original archive. Run the 27-model environment batch after all higher-priority paid models; initial completed spend is 360/1,200 credits.
- 2026-09-21 D038: Sentinel repair passes opened Idle, both Walk phases and Attack after clearing Unity's stale humanoid rest pose. The full Cantor chain is now centred through geometry, with inspected forward axes and connected body/tail pieces. Rotate all three ship intakes -90 degrees so their noses match runtime +Z traffic motion.
- 2026-09-21 D039: World pieces use centre-origin wrappers around feet-origin generated prefabs, preserve prop proportions and add explicit collision. Tile floors and walls instead of stretching a single model across an entire room. Continuous terrain and the spline shell use generated tile textures as permitted by ARCHITECTURE; generated ground slabs add local detail. Add invisible perimeter collision at walkable deck/terrain edges.
- 2026-09-21 D040: Add TallowApproach as the SpaceSafe exterior of the fifth zone, docking into the existing GroundSafe TallowDrift scene. Extend the route contract and all-zone Continue test accordingly. Survivor and Keeper reuse the generated Orrin/Hal bodies and portrait sets, as unnamed slice civilians.
- 2026-09-21 D041: First world captures exposed safe-pocket state remaining Natural after a teleport; swapping members across its boundary has the same risk. Evaluate the active member's position against the pocket volume and add a runtime teleport/swap regression. Ground encounters now spawn at ground height. NPCs initially face the approach, and attack visuals retain a readable recovery interval with wrist energy attached to hand bones.
- 2026-09-21 D042: Controlled real-hit-volume check: level-one Ridgehound takes four ordinary combo hits. At level three the +25 T2 edge reduces a stationary Burrower from 36.0 to 25.8 seconds (28.3%). Increase T2 Edge Output to +30, leaving T2 Emitter +25, to target roughly one-third at the level reached before the boss. Repeat with three actual T1 crafts followed by a crystal-consuming T2 craft; moving-boss route timing remains a separate check.
- 2026-09-21 D043: Tighten the ground camera to 19.5 m for readable hero silhouettes, reduce the vitals frame and add separate Decks/Halo/Tallow grades. First Gullet capture looked like an open flat floor: bring the spline radius to 12–15 m and move ledges/spawners inward so the generated walls define the corridor in the flight camera. New look and route evidence are required.
- 2026-09-21 D044: The 48-model true-scale row exceeded the review camera's fixed far clip and rendered uniformly blank despite exceeding the PNG byte threshold. Reject that artifact, extend far clip from actual framed bounds, and add per-channel image contrast rejection. Framed individual tiles are sound; rerender the row before closing P3.
- 2026-09-21 D045: Scope both preflight and cleanup save-writer checks to this LATTICE editor/player. An unrelated Frostbound batch editor caused the old blanket Unity check to refuse cleanup after 16 passing runtime tests. No human stash existed; archive the test-created save directory and restore the initially absent state, then rerun the complete guarded suite. Never stop the unrelated editor.
- 2026-09-21 D046: All 27 environment models succeeded for 405 credits; total paid production is 765/1,200. Upscale the five generated sky/planet panoramas with actual 4x Real-ESRGAN and resize to 4096x2048 for runtime, retaining the native 1774x887 originals. Record native and derived dimensions separately in `world-upscale.json`.
- 2026-09-21 D047: The crafted +30 T2 edge passes the controlled comparison: 36.018 seconds without it, 24.017 with it (ratio 0.6668). This uses actual T1 practice crafts, crystal-consuming T2 fabrication and melee hit volumes; stationary-target timing is not moving-boss or human-play evidence.
- 2026-09-21 D048: Final world review found ship shadows on the distant Vorun backdrop. Keep toon lighting but disable scene-shadow reception on planet-map materials and shadow casting on their renderers. Parent NPC names to their owners so Sela's name disappears when she joins; give Neve's invisible hail actor a separate sign.
- 2026-09-21 D049: The Gullet's continuous inward-facing shell already provides collision between separated generated wall modules at the flight plane. Verify this with 714 sideways physics rays across the 900 m course during every world build; reject any gap. Civil Flight retains collision bounce without damage; combat walls retain damage, covered by a runtime collision test.
- 2026-09-21 D050: The first complete-art Burrower sample took 89.5 seconds without crafted gear at 18,000 Integrity. Reduce it to 14,000 for a closer approach to the roughly one-minute target. Re-run the full route and controlled fabrication comparison; do not substitute the stationary test for moving-boss duration.
- 2026-09-21 D051: Retain the 14,000-Integrity Burrower after the final no-gear route measured 60.9 seconds; Cantor measured 109.6 seconds at 22,000. Level 6 and saved completion pass. The crafted T2 comparison is 30.5% shorter on the revised boss. Close P4 from the ordered route, opened world views and fresh 35/17 suites.
- 2026-09-21 D052: Record P5 as an automated checkpoint with final-art performance, polish, UI, saves and controller translation passing. Keep P2_CORE_OK and P5_PLAYABLE_OK open for their specified human pad-feel sessions; never convert physical attachment or scripted actions into a human-play claim. Package the playable slice and report those open gates at handoff, as P6 requires, without another approval request.
- 2026-09-21 D053: Supersede WORLD_LOOK_OK with QUALITY_REVIEW_REJECTED after playing from screenshots through ordinary virtual-gamepad input. A rendered/generated asset inventory is insufficient: sparse repeated scenery, weak silhouettes/impact and primitive menus fail the professional target. Reopen P4 visual quality and P5 presentation as well as physical feel. Preserve the old tag and evidence as historical, not current acceptance.
- 2026-09-21 D054: Add a development-only bounded input review harness. Pause GameTime between decisions; issue ordinary GamepadState events through the real input/player stack; capture each result. Never use direct combat commands, teleports or route autopilot in this review mode. Explicitly reject the initial Time.timeScale-only combat sample because its unscaled action clock kept running. Keep all review saves isolated and the original default-save state restored.
- 2026-09-21 D055: Correct defects observed through play: UI dismissal consumes buttons until release, a 0.2-second lunge buffer survives emitter recovery, combatant contact cannot apply wall damage, and the flight partner brakes only near its destination and boosts to catch up. Freeze pending combat work during pause. Add runtime regression coverage for each behavior rather than relying on route markers.
- 2026-09-21 D056: Reuse existing generated world assets for the review correction pass: quieter/smaller Decks floor modules, connected door walls, practical furnishings, Sorrel landing supplies/ridges, reduced planet coverage and a tighter flight camera. Use the already permitted CC0 particle circle for transparent warnings. Label HUD resources and expose actual bindings, skill costs and cooldowns. No new image/model generation or Meshy spend is incurred; these changes do not close the presentation rejection.
- 2026-09-21 D057: Give each smoke scenario its own explicit player log and save subdirectory. A release-title check launched alongside the route replaced the default Player.log; discard that interrupted route and rerun with independent logs. Physical-pad attachment is read from the scenario's real graphics-player log. Keep the interactive review window in the foreground so Unity does not disable its virtual input device on focus loss.
- 2026-09-23 D058: Nathan chose **Coronach** as the game name. Update Unity product metadata, title text, Windows executable names and current documentation. Keep the existing `Lattice/` Unity project, serialized asset references, internal namespaces, historical validation records and in-world Lattice terminology stable. Retain the original `Nathan/Lattice/Saves` directory so existing progress stays available after the product rename; player logs now live under `Nathan/Coronach`. Widen the title text area for the longer name without changing generated key art.

- 2026-09-23 D059: Plan Coronach around migrating living routes and a convoy of towns. The antagonist is a capable engineer enforcing a selective preservation plan. Keep the existing slice as the combat workshop; STORY_CAMPAIGN and WORLD_ATLAS are proposals, not implemented content. Target 32 main maps, four optional destinations and 22 major encounters; postpone world expansion until combat quality improves.
- 2026-09-23 D060: The reported surrounding blur is the intentional five-sample tilt-shift pass compounded by its 960x540 sample grid. Remove the blur kernel and use native camera resolution for the single-sample colour grade. Set every profile and builder to the same sharp default so regeneration cannot bring back the blur.
- 2026-09-23 D061: Reuse owned CC0 UAL1/UAL2 skeletons and animation only. Separate walk/jog/sprint and named combo/skill actions, tune playback to movement/action duration, delay damage until the wind-up and buffer one ground attack tap. Pause and defensive cancellation must prevent ghost hits. Keep visible bodies generated and document the remaining custom choreography in ANIMATION_DIRECTION.
- 2026-09-23 D062: Render review exposed wrong-facing retargeted takes and vanes detached during torso movement. Bake root orientation from the retargeted body direction. Completely unpack each generated Shaped body before parenting vanes to Chest/Hips; Unity rejects parenting into the original imported prefab. Assert that parenting actually succeeded and cover it in PlayMode.
- 2026-09-23 D063: Flight is a separate compact generated spacecraft, with the hero identity carried by palette, visor band, crest fins and wing geometry. Generate both reference images with the session image tool and both 3D hulls with the Meshy bridge. Spend 30 credits, bringing total to 795/1,200 (405 remain). Apply visual banking, roll and a 0.6-second form transition without rotating the collision body.
- 2026-09-23 D064: Route Nathan's Title Theme to the title, Hub Town Groove to town/civil areas, Moonbase Market to shops, and Adventure Awaits to Sorrel combat. Use persistent stereo playback, a 1.1-second crossfade and remembered positions; interrupted shop fades must return to the location cue. Reserve Riverbed Stomp and Seed Shakers of Xylos for the planned jungle, Shop Theme for a future merchant variant. Preserve all seven originals and avoid inventing an unrequested Gullet assignment.

- 2026-09-23 D065: The first updated full route timed out at Burrower after Taren died; preserve the red log. Add companion dodging of imminent attack telegraphs through the normal CombatActor action, and keep companion Sela at ranged distance. Do not lower boss Integrity or extend the route deadline. Re-run the combat suite and full route after this correction.
- 2026-09-23 D066: Further route diagnosis found Burrower suspended nearly two metres above ground after a charge stepped onto collision. Enemy gravity ran only in the movement branch, so waiting at ranged distance could prevent settling indefinitely. Reproduce the failure in a focused PlayMode test (expected y<0.2, actual y=2.0), then apply gravity each unpaused ground update, including telegraphs and special attacks. Keep flight planes unchanged; preserve the red XML.
- 2026-09-23 D067: Nathan requires an epic overarching story connected to most of the game. Make the Severance and Stillwater's closure of the last shared passage the explicit campaign conflict. Tie all main regions and bosses to causes, persistent consequences and later payoffs; show recruited communities participating before and during the finale. Establish Nacre and its traffic pressures before the disaster, carry the nursery from first discovery to the same final evacuation, and derive victory from learned capabilities rather than a late solution supplied by the antagonist. Most side stories travel with affected people; preserve independent local life and ecological motives. Update planning documents only, retaining the 36-map/22-boss scope and current gameplay workshop.
- 2026-09-26 D068: Nathan reports station-walking performance issues, askew combat running, weak SFX and inadequate deaths, and requests a full production plan plus a next-session prompt. Treat all reports as open; the Gullet-only uncapped benchmark and static poses do not establish station pacing or continuous motion quality. Inspection confirms commanded rather than measured ground velocity, no directional locomotion or Down/Dead animation selection, quarter-second enemy cleanup and minimal 2D SFX playback. Root causes of the reported stutter and unwanted body yaw remain unconfirmed. Add C0–C10 gates with moving station traces, continuous motion review, per-move contact/death lifecycle checks, flight tests, actual audio audition and dialogue/state checks; carry them through first-chapter and campaign production. Update documentation only this turn, preserving the existing build and 405-credit remainder. Historical SLICE_PROMPT no longer starts a new project.
- 2026-09-26 D069: Nathan requires every zone to pass a visual plausibility/eye test and reports that the first station's construction was incredulous to a viewer. Add Q07 and MAP_EYE_TEST at blockout and integrated-player stages. Require functional room/terrain relationships, usable circulation, apparent construction/support/enclosure, coherent exterior/interior scale and visible consistent fantasy rules. Explicitly redesign Hub_CinderHalo/Hub_Decks together as needed before C7; rerun performance after geometry changes. Apply the same gate throughout campaign production. Record actual reviewer evidence without inventing independent acceptance or adding a mandatory Nathan approval stop. This is a handoff-plan update, not a completed station redesign.
- 2026-09-26 D070: C0 records continuous virtual-gamepad input through the ordinary player stack, separately from the paused review driver. Require explicit isolated saves, route/scene/character/UI checks, raw elapsed intervals and unique evidence folders. Capture actual game framebuffer and listener mix; retain failed routes. Capture readback/checkpoint screenshots add overhead, so exclude them from clean timing acceptance. Unsupported GPU timing, video/audio audition and physical feel remain explicitly unverified. Baseline motion shows camera stop/go and clip-specific skeletal yaw; use independent red regressions and a calibrated import audit before correction.

- 2026-09-26 D071: Profile the station defect before intervention. Prepare Yarn's project/VM/presenter paths under the scene preparation hold; do not replace the HUD on suspicion. Preserve first-use/warm laps individually and refuse clean performance acceptance while another editor/build/encoder is active. The new visible-UI latency is queued-input-to-UI-state time, not display scanout latency.
- 2026-09-26 D072: One shared dimensional plan owns Cinder exterior/Decks interior, stable dock/spawn identities, pressure envelopes, public/service circulation and freight clearance. The standard builders delegate to this plan. Tallow's deck fits the existing refuge hull. Blockout and final-art acceptance remain separate. Baked station navigation steers the companion and development route through real doorways; CharacterController still owns displacement, with no teleport catch-up.
- 2026-09-26 D073: Correct the diagnosed donor yaw per locomotion clip (Walk 7, Run 28, Sprint 20 degrees, baked orientation), preserving actor/prefab and attack/aim axes. Actual post-collision unscaled displacement drives speed. Raw foot trajectories do not establish planted-sole quality; directional/start/stop/stride/clock work and watched motion remain open.

- 2026-09-26 D074: Generated station door frames use separate measured jamb/lintel colliders, never a box across an opening or a missing post collider. The old visible post admits a body in the red test; corrected collision and rebaked companion navigation pass. Preserve the dock/spawn and quest identities while adjusting the physical envelope.
- 2026-09-26 D075: Reject impossible work-counter durations even when their p95 passes. Keep raw GPU values and the originally mistaken summary, then emit a corrected rejection. A reported unavailable counter is not zero work, and a playing AudioSource count is not a simultaneous one-shot voice count. Separate clean traversal, headroom, capture and optional object/source census evidence.
- 2026-09-26 D076: Calibrate stride against visible generated sole geometry after retargeting. Contact drift alone misses intersecting legs; require independent shin clearance and actual runtime mesh probes as well. Directional corrections remain experimental until those checks and pose review pass. Assisted attack targeting is distinct from explicit toggleable target-facing movement. Preserve positive global animation/action clocks when using a negative locomotion state multiplier for back steps.

- 2026-09-26 D077: Visible generated floor grilles need recessed generated pressure backing; tint alone cannot close geometric openings. Keep walking height and collision unchanged and verify the rotated backing geometry in actual renders. Extend clean-timing competitor detection to other Unity players while leaving their processes untouched.
- 2026-09-26 D078: Never compare a few instantaneous shoulder samples with the complete-cycle torso target. Preserve the mistaken report, require whole contiguous phase coverage, and extend ordinary routes in real clear circulation. Starts/stops use short bounded physical acceleration/braking with actual displacement driving stride; dash remains its explicit action.

- 2026-09-26 D079: Motion evidence must segment animation restarts, clip/step changes and transitions. Repeated cycle-zero contacts in separate strides are not one foot plant. Integrate a complete contiguous phase period from any starting phase; preserve original mistaken summaries. Framebuffer recording uses a bounded reusable pool to avoid imposing hundreds of MB/s of managed allocation on the motion under review.

- 2026-09-26 D080: Evaluate corrected heel/toe clearance against the local support plane at their final positions. Solve both ankle targets before a bounded pelvis adjustment and leg IK. Release Walk contact during authored toe-off instead of holding an unreachable trailing foot or increasing the pelvis-drop bound. Keep flat/directional, actual rendered ramp, paused and failed-control evidence separate. Companion formation uses leader-relative right; a world-right offset collapsed east-facing separation.

- 2026-09-26 D081: Lethal gameplay resolution cancels owner-bound pending contacts immediately, while visual finish and cleanup use a separate lifecycle. Preserve Scrapmite's named death-attack exception and already-launched projectile flight. Downed heroes keep a recoverable body, then a protected get-up; delay defeat pause until the collapse can finish. Reuse owned skeleton-only Down/Revive clips, preserving generated visible geometry and existing asset GUIDs.
- 2026-09-26 D082: A scaled mesh sibling makes naive BakeMesh-plus-renderer-transform previews misleading. Use bone-matrix skinning for such generated rig geometry. Prebake per-body lifecycle support curves and vane folding in intake; do not place a fallen body from hip height alone or scan its entire mesh every live frame. Actual in-player support and motion review remain separate from isolated pose checks.

- 2026-09-26 D083: Reserve backward playback, limited lower-body strafing and upper-body counter-twist for explicit target lock. Free traversal follows actual measured motion, including collision redirection; a nearby obstacle must not silently turn free travel into aimed backpedalling. Keep the failing ordinary replay and independent 180-degree red regression.

- 2026-09-26 D084: Melee contact follows the rendered wrist edge and its swept active phase after animation, with one victim set and owner/action cancellation. An explicit trigger capsule owns each body damage envelope; the movement controller query shape must not silently shorten it. Preserve rejection of the legacy oversized sphere, the same contact-distance bound and the same resumed-hit deadline. Visible weapon reach, companion approach and authored phases must agree.

- **D085 - Explicit reach requires explicit combat fixtures.** The old kill test sometimes placed the fighter inside the arena crystal behind an enemy that had moved during scene setup. Controller depenetration left a 2.2765 m gap and solid cover; the old sphere hid that setup error. Keep the actual motor, enemy, five-second timeout and kill assertions, restore full health and use the clear southern lane. Separate ordinary attacks must miss covered/retreating targets and hit the same unobstructed body; injected legacy spheres demonstrate that those checks reject the old behavior. No damage multiplier, blade range or geometry bound is relaxed.

- 2026-09-26 D086: Interior launch points need a visible pressure boundary and a shared exterior/interior footprint. Replace continuous wall behind the Cinder hatch with a real framed opening. Align Tallow's five-metre lift cabin (interior z=-12..-7, exterior z=3..8), extend its shaft into the occupied deck, and attach the apron ahead of it. Keep zone/spawn identities while moving the Dock location to z=0.5; update the ordinary flight approach accordingly. Closed service cells flank the lower cabin. Do not accept editor views as final in-player MAP_EYE_TEST evidence.

- 2026-09-26 D087: A connected floor plan does not excuse a visible gap below the floor. The extended close hatch views expose the Cinder casing top at -0.5 m and bridge support at -1 m beneath a -0.16 m floor underside. Extend both to y=0, overlapping the existing walking deck while retaining its level, ring contact, doors, docks and routes. Preserve final-docks-03 as a functional pass but spatial rejection, then recapture the join.

- 2026-09-26 D088: Ground ranged releases sample Sela's posed emitter wrist after animation, with explicit phase data and owner/action cancellation. Aim at the target body height so attachment correction does not make short targets unhittable. Scatter Bloom retains five projectiles from one release; Thread Lance uses one visible beam path, stops at the nearest static cover and pierces each exposed body once. Preserve the ordinary-origin, fan-origin and covered-lance red evidence. Reusing the existing ranged take is an attachment correction, not acceptance of the complete authored moveset or its observed feel.

- 2026-09-26 D089: Moving shots must retain actual leg stride. The whole-body firing take moves a rendered sole only 1.8 mm relative to a travelling body in the red fixture. Use a masked Sela upper-body layer with its own action-rate multiplier, leaving the base locomotion clock and grounded feet intact. Contact/shot telemetry follows the action layer; stride telemetry follows the base layer. Preserve both existing controller GUIDs and maintain the setup in intake. Keep a layered-pose pause regression and actual sole geometry bounds, rather than accepting a layer name as evidence.

- 2026-09-26 D090: Preserve a refused PlayMode save restoration as a hard stop for subsequent PlayMode launches. Never reclassify leftover fixture saves as the human originals. Record writer identity on cleanup refusal, verify original hashes before recovery, and quarantine fixtures. The first refusal did not record its writer identity; do not attribute it to the separate project without evidence.

- 2026-09-26 D091: Accept the final Cinder pair spatially only after the unchanged three-dock circuit and complete interior loop both retain focus and pass, with actual opened hatch/room/approach images and unlabelled exterior/interior comparisons. Keep capture overhead and the active foreign player separate from C1 clean timing. This closes the station spatial defect, not the all-map, continuous-motion or professional presentation gates.

- 2026-09-26 D092: A three-second break invalidates the old attack, rather than merely suspending AI until its tell has expired. Cancel pending contact volumes and enemy/boss attack work immediately; recovery must start a fresh wind-up. Preserve already-launched projectiles and the explicit Scrapmite death attack. Prove both cancellation and that retained projectile boundary with real health/contact tests.

- 2026-09-26 D093: Hold an inspected owned recoil pose for Sentinel break and preserve its generated body. Ground support must use the rendered mesh's lowest surface and ignore combatant colliders; an enemy's own capsule is not a floor. Keep the penetrating-pose and self-support failures, add bounded world-height and rendered-sole checks, and cross-check the scaled sibling rig with both bone-matrix skinning and BakeMesh before trusting either probe.

- 2026-09-26 D094: A non-penetrating lowest point does not establish a settled corpse. Preserve the ordinary-player floating-body rejection and add terminal pelvis/ankle bounds with side views. Settle Sentinel's retargeted pose gradually around its hip pivot using a bounded mesh-derived tilt, rebake support, and verify actual geometry on flat/sloped floors without moving the gameplay root. Do not apply Sentinel's correction indiscriminately to the heroes.

- 2026-09-26 D095: Ordinary Sentinel impacts use a short masked torso recoil, preserving the base leg stride; armor reduces its amplitude and break/death override it. Author the missing spine bend on the owned take after the donor-only masked attempt fails. Keep the exposed/armored pair, real mesh floor and matched walking tests. Drive the persistent armor cue from actual protective state, and notify generated animators immediately on pause so late input cannot leave a queued animation advance.

- 2026-09-26 D096: Ground skill contact follows the posed move and the travelled world path. Ember Dash must not damage along movement denied by collision; Pulse damage must arrive with its visible crest; Static Net must travel from the throwing hand and stop at cover within a bounded range. Released energy can finish after interruption, while pending actions cancel. Opaque cover clips persistent field graphics as well as damage. Calibrate the owned particle texture's bright crest rather than treating its padded image bounds as the gameplay radius. Preserve the hidden-contact, field-over-wall, texture-padding and material-initialization failures.

- 2026-09-26 D097: Ground Overdrive and Refract arm from their own observed cast phase, with persistent wrist cues tied to actual buff state. Refract interception must initiate the return-shot pose, not hidden immediate damage. A deliberate guard/dodge replaces the armed counter, preserving Flash Guard/Move. The returning beam respects opaque movable props as well as static walls; sweep the body-to-muzzle segment so a sampled wrist cannot fire from beyond near cover. Preserve original duration, bonus, cost/cooldown and the deferred C4 flight branch.

- **D098 - Released energy keeps its collision and team contract (September 26).** Resolve nearest opaque surface/body independent of query order, include movable solid props, snapshot faction before source cleanup, and decrement active accounting on disable/unload. Pending action cancellation does not erase already released energy. Red regressions and unchanged positive controls precede the correction; full integrated validation remains pending after the17-check source checkpoint.

- **D099 - Ground collapse needs support throughout the fall and a believable resting attitude (September 26).** Cache the actual frozen lethal-pose geometry once, choose solid floor support without combatants, and support every intermediate rotation on the floor normal. Do not move gameplay roots or tie rewards to cleanup. Preserve a second rejection for bodies balanced on paw/leg/jaw after clearance passes; choose each species' resting attitude from actual geometry plus opened front/side images, and independently bound rendered centroid height. Rigid limbs and unreviewed flight failure remain explicit C3 work; direct-lethal diagnostic footage cannot stand in for ordinary input.

- **D100 - Sorrel must remain a connected working basin through saved and sealed states (September 26).** Rebuild its terrain and outpost in isolation, preserving identifiers and existing saves. Ground interactions at measured mesh bounds and require reachable standing space within the actual prompt range. Carry membrane state into carved companion navigation. Trace rejected bypass paths before reshaping cliffs; do not hide them with disconnected navmesh islands. Keep the first foreground-occlusion/material/rectangular-cut rejections and review actual generated door directions before final replacement. Reset interrupted seal presentation together with collision/navigation. Grade the full landing apron beneath shallow generated pads. A successful outpost walk is partial map evidence, not all-zone or full-game acceptance.

- **D101 - Match flight distance across frame durations before tuning feel (September 26).** Keep the existing acceleration, drag and speed caps; integrate constant thrust with drag and accumulate average-velocity travel in steps no longer than 1/120 second. Preserve the original 30/60/120 braking failure and distinguish numerical checks from actual observed circuits. The corrected 60 fps brake travels about 9.8 cm farther because the old end-velocity update discarded distance. Zero-time calls must preserve boosted momentum. Visual/physical flight acceptance remains separate.

- **D102 - A flight dash can contact only the space its craft reaches (September 27).** Replace frame-count damage placements with segments reported after CharacterController movement. Reuse the original attack radius and packet, check static/movable cover and deduplicate victims per action. Use the same path for Taren's flight dash skill. Preserve all three original lunge failures and the separate skill-through-wall failure. Drive the trail from travelled distance; a passing collision test does not establish observed ship motion or effect quality.

- **D103 - Captured audio limits require a rejecting measurement path (September 27).** Retain original mixes, unique output folders, raw FFmpeg logs and source/tool hashes. Reject silence, missing summaries, invalid audio and rounded values that cannot establish margin under the existing true-peak ceiling. Real PCM positive/negative controls and the reproduced rounding defect validate the checker only; numerical levels do not establish an auditioned sound palette or mix. Preserve music and cue assignments.

- **D104 - Interrupted form requests preserve the visible body (September 27).** Coalesce requests for the same incoming visual, resize from the current scale and reverse by growing the still-visible body. Civil/Combat flight updates rules without resetting the shared hull. Retain both the original scale-pop failures and the incoming-flight retarget restart. Ordinary boundary routes must cross the pivot-based safe bounds; high-speed input that stays outside cannot prove interruption. Keep the full 104/44 integration result separate from the still-open staged transformation and observed flight quality.

- **D105 - Ship effects attach to measured hull features and actual commands (September 27).** Sample nose releases after visible bank/roll, retain independently rolled fan packets and sweep the body-to-muzzle gap for near cover. Use the same visible cover-limited path for flight lance/counter. Measure and open engine sockets against actual nozzle geometry; preserve rejection of Sela's misplaced centre nozzle. Reusable attached exhaust follows commanded thrust, not retained velocity, and respects pause/disable without spawning repeated objects. Socket renders and state regressions do not establish observed player effect quality.

- **D106 - Keep sound preparation reproducible and distinguish selection hypotheses from audition (September 27).** Build small owned-source derivatives with explicit layer/gain/rate/delay recipes and unchanged-original/licence hashes. Refuse reused output directories; keep candidates outside Unity until their runtime integration. Numerical silence/peak checks support asset integrity, not sound character or mix acceptance. Preserve music originals and current cues; missing listening capability remains UNVERIFIED while implementation continues.

- **D107 - A generated craft needs a hull-sized physical footprint (September 27).** Replace its humanoid capsule with a stable sphere measured around the visual bank pivot; restore cached ground geometry on landing. Match the damage body, arrival separation and adjacent revive reach to those dimensions. Preserve the deliberate wider vulnerability for ordinary combat review. Lunge contact must meet its visible edge, including stroke thickness across the controller skin gap. Replace an obsolete centre-position fixture with actual collision callbacks, retaining damage assertions and proving actor-as-wall damage still rejects. A successful transform attachment check cannot accept invisible texture padding; retain the opened effect rejections and explicit UV correction.

- **D108 - Judge flight effects at the actual gameplay scale (September 27).** Preserve the full 118/48 integration pass and successful short Gullet contact routes, then retain the ordinary exhaust-readability rejection despite passing enlarged attachment renders. A successful dock does not erase focus loss or unreachable approach goals from a whole circuit. Correct the goals to observed pad clearance, keep the same input/focus requirements, and document the wider damage body's actual combat consequence. Exposed ring-panel edge clutter remains visual cleanup, not a newly accepted station view.

- **D109 - Flight skills release visible energy and preserve its plane and team (September 27).** Replace Taren's hidden flight volumes with a forward 130-degree Cleave and full Pulse whose contact follows the expanding edge. Bound Sela's nose-launched net to six metres, sweep cover and retain flight altitude without changing the ground throw. Released seeds retain their captured faction even after source destruction. Buffs anticipate before arming and display actual-state hull cues. Preserve baseline failures, deliberate behind-wave/floor faults and the real cleanup friendly-fire failure. Brighter energy must still pass ordinary gameplay review; enlarged controlled renders alone cannot accept it.

- **D108 - Folding keeps full-size identity and owns its short control window (September 27).** Tuck the existing humanoid rig and attached generated vanes, temporarily fold the existing ship wing vertices, and restore their exact rest geometry after the exchange. Retain repeat/reverse continuity without scale collapse. Docking waits for departure folding and opens the arriving body during fade-in. Cancel pending actions and motor travel, and reject new movement until unfolding ends. Enable mesh readback only for the two flight imports. Controlled stills and regression passes establish the candidate geometry/state behavior; ordinary exchange readability and continuous motion remain separate acceptance work.

- **D109 - Recoverable craft keep space and a visible rescue state (September 27).** Disable their damage trigger while retaining a stationary hull obstacle; existing assistance reach already accommodates separate hulls. Use a bounded settling pose, emergency wing fold and reusable rescue ring, then restore from the actual current pose. Pause holds the last rendered state. Ground/flight boundaries must remove and restore the matching cue and collision, including for a downed partner. Preserve the overlap, early-revive jump, pause drift and stale landing-ring rejections. Controlled geometry acceptance does not replace gameplay-distance or continuous-motion review.

- **D110 - Later traversal can reopen an accepted map view (September 27).** The C4 station perimeter exposes stretched, ragged ring construction missed by the earlier final station inspection. Reopen the paired final MAP_EYE_TEST, retain the interior circulation evidence and compare a normally proportioned generated box section before changing release geometry. Keep interrupted captures rejected and leave unrelated Frostbound processes untouched. A corrected route expectation needs its own repeat; do not rewrite a failed capture into a pass.

- **D111 - The Cinder ring is a box section of native-scale modules (September 28).** Keep the 62 m radius, 4 × 5 m section and y=-3.5 contact with the hull casings. Build 96 spans, each a generated deck plate over inner and outer 3.5 m wall fascias. These are the hull roof and wall modules near their native proportions, so the ring shares the hulls' construction language instead of lying one wall panel on its back. Meet plates at the outer vertex and fascias at their exposed-face vertices; alternate plates 2 cm to avoid coplanar overlap flicker. Leave the unseen underside open. `StationRedesign.Exterior` rebuilds only the flight exterior, leaving the walkable Decks and its navigation untouched. Controlled views are not the final pass; an ordinary in-player perimeter recapture is still required.

- **D112 - Combat and menu cues report what actually happened (September 28).** A melee contact window plays a swing; impacts come only from damage actually resolved in `Health.Receive`, so a miss never sounds like a hit. A hit sounds armored when the body is shielded and unbroken, the damage type is resisted, a hero is guarding, or the target is a ship hull, matching the enemy recoil rule. At most three impacts start in any 50 ms window, so one wide skill cannot stack a crowd into one peak. Each hero keeps a distinct needle family on ground and in flight; hostile fire, previously silent, uses a lower, heavier shot so it never reads as the player's own. Menu buttons play confirm only after an action that reported neither error nor cancel. Refused purchases, crafts and gear operations play the error cue, Back and B play cancel, navigation between two live controls plays move, and dialogue advance plays a tick. Checks run red against the previous runtime before the wiring is accepted.

- **D113 - Bodies voice their own defeat, and defence has its own sounds (September 28).** An enemy's death cue plays at the body and follows what it is. Sentinel and Mine archetypes fail mechanically, and every other non-boss falls as a creature. The former generic crunch at the killer is removed. Bosses keep that crunch until each has a designed finish, and Cantor's release must never borrow a creature death. A downed hero hits the deck on the ground, or fails like a machine in flight; revival rises. A ground dodge is a cloth-and-air body movement, while the flight roll keeps its thruster. Refract interception rings distinctly. Flash Move keeps its force-field cue. Staging accepts named palette sets, each with its own provenance record; the original set must still carry exactly 32 clips.

- **D114 - Music fills silence without overriding Nathan's assignments (September 28).** The four existing cues stay. Space combat zones that had no music get Starfight, and boss encounters override the zone cue with Alien Boss Battle from arrival until defeat or removal. Builds stage only the tracks the slice plays; every other supplied original stays in `Music/` with a committed hash and planned cue until its map exists. MP3 downloads and duplicate conversions stay out of git. Loop points for fading Suno endings wait for listening.

- **D115 - Draft 3 world expansion (September 28).** Nathan's requested regions join the Severance rather than sitting beside it. The Vaskar Muster, a warmongering people's war-state, is a third answer to the departing routes: caging young Choir as war-roads. It sharpens Meret's argument rather than replacing her as the final antagonist. The Ancelane are the civilisation of the previous Severance. Lanternwick (ancient town) and Oriel (near-magical world, with one visible fibre rule) supply history and a release principle, never a relic solution. Wendmire (swamp, Driftreed), Hoarfell (ice, Lastlight) and Duskline (the moving Meridian Crawl) each provide a convoy capability used in the finale. Six new space areas each carry a distinct rule. Maps 01–36 keep their numbers; new maps are 37–57.

- **D116 - Corpses leave, fliers fall, partners cover (September 28).** No defeated body disappears at full size. After its hold the whole root settles and shrinks over 0.45 s with a puff, inside the existing lifecycle bounds. Killed fliers lose lift and fall below the flight plane instead of hovering. A swap forced by a downed hero uses a cover line; banter belongs to the player's own swaps.

- **D117 - The Gullet is anatomy, built from one profile (September 28).** Replace the repeated sinusoid with a mouth, entry canal, feeding chamber, sphincter valves, a slalom throat with tissue folds and a salvage eddy, a nursery gate chamber, the Cantor's 76 m coil and an exit valve, all defined in `GulletProfile`. Keep every encounter, membrane, spawn, cache and flag identity so saves and quests are unaffected. One generated membrane texture is tinted per organ, changing only at valves. Folds are placed by their measured tip so the travel line keeps at least 6.7 m clearance. The builder, smoke route, circuit generator and layout tests all read the same profile. A focus-rejected capture stays rejected; the ordinary flight pass waits for a quiet machine rather than relaxing the focus rule.

- **D118 - Nathan's answers on Draft 3 and assets (September 28).** (1) Keep the Vaskar Muster as a third force, with Meret as final antagonist (Option A). (2) The 40–46-hour scope is accepted, and side quests remain part of it. (3) Music rule: **Hub Town Groove** is the town music, including flying around a station and talking to people in other ships. The space exploration tracks (Starfield Frontier, Starward Journey, Endless Flight) play outside hub towns and other space-based towns, and Starfight plays in space combat. The current assignments already follow this: Cinder Halo and the Tallow approach are towns, and the Gullet is combat. (4) No loop-point editing: tracks loop immediately at their end. (5) Nathan authorised his full Meshy balance, 4,157 credits confirmed live on 2026-09-28. The bridge cap is now 4,952 cumulative including the earlier 795, and Meshy's text-to-image and image-to-image now produce the reference boards and isolated references that previously needed an external image tool. The art rules are unchanged: generate only what a map or encounter actually needs, board first, isolated references QA'd before any 3D spend, and nothing accepted until seen in a render.

- **D119 - Hushwell is built to the fixed camera, and its one rule bites both ways (September 28).** The cave is a single heightfield whose rock never rises into the sightline from the ground camera (40°/20°/19.5 m, 30° FOV) to a hero standing on any floor. Rock height is measured from the lower level when a ramp shoulder overlooks the next chamber, and a steep lip of at least 1.5 m keeps heroes off the rock. Generated grooved faces stand only where the whole 5 m slab lies in rock below every sightline beyond it. West walls stay bare, stepped rock by this rule; the map is asymmetric rather than hiding its heroes. A sweep of the floor from the camera's own pose is a standing play test and must run on final art, because thinner blockout stand-ins passed where the final slabs failed. The nursery's breath scalds hounds as well as heroes, so the vents are terrain to fight around rather than a timer to wait out. The Bellows is braced by two Kinetic-weak pressure organs, making Taren the organ breaker. Each exhale also blasts out through one pumping organ, in turn, across that organ's half of the chamber, so breaking one organ chooses the safe half, as the atlas's B02 design asks. The final model is a limbless domed bellows whose breathing is its animation, so it needs no rig. Below 30 % it crosses the chamber between four exposed ribs toward the party, completing the atlas design. Hushwell stays optional off the Burrower's drill site until the chapter-one quest is rewritten, so saves and the warp-key route are untouched.

- **D120 - The practice scenes are places with a protected working core (September 29).** The ground arena becomes an outpost maintenance proving yard and the flight arena a dockside ship proving berth, per the September 26 briefs. Both keep the arrival, opponent spawns, Hal's dialogue and a clear core (|x| < 10, −18 < z < 10), matching where the old scatter began, so the combat and motion tests keep their space. Structure marks every collision edge: rock, panels and crates on the ground; rails and posts at the flight plane. Hal stands out of the firing path in the yard and speaks from a supported cabin at the berth, with no humanoid on the flight plane. `ArenaLayoutTests` rejected both old scenes before the rebuild.

- **D121 - Optional places carry their own quest and objective (September 29).** A side area starts its quest the first time it is entered (`QuestStarter`), so a player who wanders in never lacks an objective, and killing or discovering there always counts. Hushwell's quest completes on the Bellows kill and the nursery discovery. Speakers gain a later conversation tier (`Npc.finalFlag`/`finalNode`), checked before the post-quest node, so the Survivor can acknowledge the nursery without new dialogue functions. Every zone must have an objective HUD case; the fall-through practice text is for the arenas only. Enemy bodies that are not rigged are imported readable, because the rigid death placement reads their vertices.

- **D122 - Station residents speak from their ships (September 29).** Following Nathan's music rule, the station's town is also a place of hailed conversations between vessels. New ship-borne speakers have no standing body and nothing that collides; parked ships never enter a recorded route. The first two carry Act I's planned seeds: an Anchor Compact surveyor and a household bound for the Nacre transfer. Speakers without a generated body must not keep the placeholder capsule, whose collider is an invisible obstacle.

- **D123 - A player must be able to do it, not nearly (September 30).** Ordinary-input circuits are the judge of the Gullet and the docks, and they overturned three things that controlled checks had accepted:
  - **Docks.** A flight dock's prompt reaches past where a hull physically stops against its pad from every open side. Flight docks use 6.5 m; ground pads keep 4 m.
  - **Tunnel walls.** The tunnel's collision edge is its shell. Decorative ribs may lap it from outside but never stand in the passage, measured across their whole footprint, because on a bend a straight slab's ends meet a different edge than its centre.
  - **The partner.** The AI partner reads incoming fire the way a player does and rolls or sidesteps across any hostile shot about to hit, in flight and on the ground; it had been standing under spitter fire.

  The scripted slice smoke may be taught ordinary pilot sense (waypoints, stances, keeping range from mines), but its marker contract is never loosened.

- **D124 - Swarms are weak one by one, and fixtures are reachable saves (September 30).** Hushwell's first ordinary descent overturned two things no controlled check had exercised:
  - **Scrapmite swarms.** The Scrapmite had shared the Ridgehound's full `Bite` (18), so six "weak" mites bit the active hero twice as hard as a three-hound pack (46/s against 23/s) before their death bursts. The Scrapmite now has its own `Nip` (7 damage, 0.6 s tell, 1.8 s cooldown, 2.4 m reach): a swarm bites no harder than a hound pack in the same zone. The death burst stays as the swarm's punishment for staying adjacent (D081, D092).
  - **Development loadouts.** A loadout stands in for a real save, so it must be a state a player can reach. The warp key comes only from the anvil the Burrower guards, so the `gullet` party has met the survivor, killed the Burrower and cleared its arena. Hushwell's lift had returned the party into a living Burrower's arena.
  - **Replay input.** A route may chase its hero's current target (`approachTarget`, with `rangedTolerance` for Sela, who shoots from range). It holds the stick toward the target whenever the target breaks away, and swings only in reach. It may end a step when the target it locked at the start falls (`until: targetDown`), and cut a fight round short on its outcome (`stopWhen`, which unlike `until` is not a requirement). All three are stick and button input through the ordinary Input System, never teleports; each fight's outcome is still asserted by its flag.

- **D125 - Every speaker has their own face (September 30).** This supersedes D040's portrait reuse: the Survivor and the Keeper get their own generated portrait sets, so the Sorrel survivor no longer speaks with Orrin's face. A speaker may never show another speaker's set, and every tagged emotion must exist in the set rather than falling back to Neutral. Dialogue type renders at 18 px or more, and hints at 16 px, at the smallest supported size (1280×720). Glyphs stay clear of the frame border. The Survivor's and Keeper's bodies still reuse Orrin's and Hal's models until they are generated.

- **D126 - Open questions settled (September 30).** Nathan asked for the best available decision on each open question, so development is not held for them:
  - **Bellows tuning stays.** Every attack is telegraphed and avoidable. The replay's no-dodge run is the floor: Taren falls in the last phase and Sela finishes from range. Revisit only after a person plays it.
  - **Scrapmite `Nip` stays** at 7 damage, 0.6 s tell and 1.8 s cooldown (D124).
  - **The arena motion fixture retires.** `c0-motion-arena_ground` remains as C0 baseline history, not a regression fixture. The proving yard's sparring opponents stand 5 m from the arrival by contract, so an idle 8-second settle there measures being mauled, not motion. C2 motion evidence rests on the `c2-*` routes, which start in quiet spaces on the Decks and at the Sorrel outpost.
  - **Decks GPU headroom gets an independent source.** Unity's `FrameTimingManager` is enabled in the players and recorded beside the profiler counter. If both agree, the headroom is judged; if they disagree, the disagreement is the finding.
  - **Button hints follow the active device.** Dialogue and prompt hints name the pad buttons or the keys, whichever was used last, not both at once.
  - **Survivor and Keeper bodies are generated** like their faces (D125), so no speaking character borrows another's body (C8's no-stand-in rule).
  - **Music loops stay immediate** (Nathan's rule); tracks that end in a long fade dip before restarting. Choosing loop points waits for his ear, and listening stays UNVERIFIED.
  - **Hushwell stays an optional side area** until C8's chapter rewrite. The rewrite makes the nursery discovery part of the main story, as STORY_CAMPAIGN requires (the nursery is tracked from Sorrel to the finale).
  - **Remaining ordinary routes come first**, before new content: the walk into Hushwell from Sorrel, Sorrel's service branch, and the Gullet's gate, coil and exit. After them comes the C7 release flow from New Game to Tallow by agent-directed virtual input, recorded as such and not as human play.

- **D127 - Nathan's purchased water and grass packs are approved (September 30).** Staggart Creations' **Stylized Water 3** (3.2.7) and **Stylized Grass Shader** (2.1.0, for Unity 6) are paid packs Nathan bought for Coronach. They are an explicit exception to the generated-art policy (D001): use them wherever they lift the look of land and water. Both target URP 17 on Unity 6000.0+, and the project runs URP 17.4 on 6000.4.
  - **Licensing:** the repo is public, so the packs' source never enters git. `.gitignore` excludes `Lattice/Assets/Stylized Water 3/` and the embedded `Lattice/Packages/xyz.staggart-creations.stylized-grass/`. They are imported locally from `%APPDATA%\Unity\Asset Store-5.x\Staggart Creations\Shaders\`.
  - **Open:** scenes and materials that reference them are committed. A fresh clone without the packs must still open, build and pass its tests, or the gap must be documented with exact import steps; the first integration decides which.
  - Their stylized look must sit with the HD-2D toon treatment, the fixed-camera sightline rule and the C1 frame budgets.

- **D128 - A flight replay re-aims after rolling; failure must preserve its report (October 3).** The first ordinary Cantor route fired sideways after its lateral rolls: a flight chase had stopped steering once within range, even though rolling changes the nose. `approachTarget` now makes a brief 0.25 stick correction when a flight actor is more than five degrees off its target, releasing once aligned. This uses the ordinary Input System and clears its dead zone; no actor rotation, aim assistance or combat tuning changes. The projectile-damage regression fails when that correction is disabled. An absent party at checkpoint validation records rejection instead of throwing and losing the report, also proven red before green. Sorrel's service/bore and the full Gullet exit routes now pass ordinary input; the opened Cantor views expose an off-screen-target camera defect, so route success does not close C4/MAP visual acceptance. [Evidence](validation/MAP-remaining-routes.md).

- **D129 - Enable and retain both Unity GPU timing access paths (October 3).** Enable Frame Timing Stats in Player Settings for release as well as development. Headroom replays record `FrameTimingManager`'s raw GPU/CPU durations and original frame-start timestamp beside the profiler counters. Cached frames are deduplicated by timestamp, never by duration; pending, invalid and impossible readings remain explicit. Require at least 90% distinct positive API GPU readings, the existing 14 ms p95 budget, and profiler/API p95 agreement within 0.25 ms or 10%, whichever is larger. These are separate access paths to Unity's shared timing backend, not independent hardware clocks. Both clean Decks builds now agree without the earlier impossible readings: GPU p95 1.855 ms release / 1.935 ms development. This closes D126's measurement gap, without claiming the old counter fault's root cause or continuous human observation. [Evidence](validation/C1-station-performance.md#frametimingmanager-cross-check-october-3-codex).

- **D130 - Speaker identity includes the body baked into the map (October 3).** Survivor and Keeper now use independent generated T2 bodies, matched to their portraits, with existing owned animation clips. A definition-only body check missed the old NPC prefab baked into Sorrel; scene-level mesh checks now enforce the same identity in Sorrel and Tallow. Use isolated visual-child replacement rather than rebuilding those maps. Remove the retired Orrin/Hal alias assignment from definition setup. Both normal-input dialogue recaptures pass and show the new bodies. Cost: 40 credits, live remaining balance 2,823. [Evidence](validation/C8-speaker-bodies.md).

- **D131 - Paid landscape art is an optional local layer (October 3).** Public scenes serialize our generated fallback grass/water, collision and navigation only. Licensed shaders, textures, combined grass meshes, cloned materials and renderer settings live in ignored roots; runtime resource names select local overrides without any vendor C# dependency. A no-pack clone remains playable and uses the same saves. Keep vegetation below 0.6 m and out of combat/interaction lanes; nursery pools occupy actual shallow basins, while Tallow water belongs to a contained recycling bed. The fixed ground camera stays unchanged. The private pipeline retains HD-2D processing and disables bending, water height prepass and directional caustics. Rebuild private overrides after public surface changes, keep the automatically discovered embedded grass entry out of the public package lock, and measure the private pipeline in both build configurations before C1 acceptance. Later swamp/ice/volcanic palettes remain map-specific work. [Setup and boundary](OPTIONAL_ART_PACKS.md).

- **D132 - Cancelling a conversation is not completing it (October 3).** Back and Pause do not leave an active story line or choice; finish it or choose its explicit leave option. A tap reveals, a fresh tap advances, and a held confirm never repeats. Shop/bench handoffs retain focus and consume their opening/closing input. Internal stop/unload must return no choice, drain pending presenter continuations before Yarn disposes its cancellation state, and suppress completion-only quest credit, joins and UI requests. Tests reproduced a default-first-option selection, a stopped-VM selection exception, and aborted Survivor quest credit before these changes. Eleven focused tests exercise every compiled story node/choice with AutoAdvance off, reload branch state and verify once-only gifts/joins/rewards; presentation and physical input evidence remain separately reported. The full-suite integration check then exposed early completion and unloaded-view callbacks: Running now includes the presenter/command drain, and presenters release disappearing views.

- **D133 - A combat replay can react to a visible attack tell (October 3).** An opt-in `evadeTelegraphs` step sends lateral stick and East during the chased enemy's final wind-up. It releases between tells and suspends the step's attack buttons during evasion. It calls no actor actions and changes no damage, timing, difficulty or outcome assertion. The post-landscape Hushwell recapture lost both heroes while its stationary chase repeatedly took the enlarged phase-three breath; preserve that rejection. The new regression first failed without a dodge, then proved ordinary-input defence and release across two tells. With evasive input, the full descent, Bellows, nursery and return lift pass in 410.8 seconds. This is agent-directed replay evidence, not a physical-controller difficulty verdict.
- **D134 - Flight encounters fit visible bodies without changing the ground view (October 3).** The active hero and a living locked target within 30 m provide a world-space envelope through a generic Core camera bridge. Fit the visible meshes and hero hull with a safe viewport margin, preserving the authored pitch, yaw and FOV; ease distance and return to travel framing when the target leaves. Cache renderer discovery, exclude transient telegraphs, and retain the current composition when swapping heroes in the same encounter. The old camera and swap reset both fail the actual Cantor geometry check at 17 m; removing the ground guard also fails its isolation test. Ordinary Gullet recapture accepts combat visibility, not the coil's still-weak floor/clamp composition or the unimplemented collar-release story.

Preserve every old assertion, failed fixture and capture. Three deliberate variants reject leakage, persistent suppression and gameplay during UI. The identical earned route now passes after full 173/339 verification; its return endpoint improves from 16.08 m to 0.50 m from the goal, without a replay-only trigger release. The 61.1-second fight and sampled visual quality remain unaccepted. [Evidence](validation/C8-cantor-preview.md).

- **D181 - Attribute released shots before changing their balance (October 8).** Add capture-owned launch and volley identities, with source identity and release geometry copied into each actual projectile. Preserve identity through pool reuse and source destruction; retain impact-time liveness separately. Group real enemy and hero fans without altering their damage or survival. Keep the producer bounded and inactive outside recorded capture. Independent versioned validation must reject missing or inconsistent new provenance, while older captures remain explicitly unable to identify shots. Preserve all original fixture, checker and player failures. New attribution may explain new impacts; it cannot retroactively identify the earlier unrecorded volleys or accept balance. [Evidence](validation/C4-damage-provenance.md).

- **D182 - Give the departing animal room through real companion movement (October 8).** Keep bounded body guidance after lethal target resolution, reacquire it across a hero swap, and release cached geometry on recovery or actual removal. A new live target may coexist with one departing animal. Frame traces show that current bounds alone route across an accelerating nose; reserve five envelopes along the next 1.5 seconds of its authored path, using the evaluated presentation clock. Forecasts guide the route; only current anatomy triggers braking, because braking against a future envelope let the animal catch a slow escape. Keep pre-LateUpdate measurements and add a separate post-presentation sampler, with rejecting controls. Preserve the animal's trajectory, full size, collision shutdown, 3.6-second lifetime and every old assertion. Original overlap, stale-reference, phase-cache, compiler and failed-candidate evidence remains. Numerical clearance and selected images cannot accept continuous motion or retrospectively explain an older screenshot. [Evidence](validation/C4-release-spacing.md).

- **D183 - Treat one Drifter fan as one damaging burst per hero hull (October 8).** Preserve all three actual projectiles and use an attack-owned hull budget after immunity and positive-damage checks. Different heroes and later/independent volleys retain their own damage; released shots survive source death. Preserve the original three-hit failures, generic-Health provenance witness, ineffective pause fault and all rejecting controls. The repair reduces an attributed close-range stack; it does not accept overall balance. [Evidence](validation/C4-drifter-fan.md).

- **D184 - Record evaluated release geometry before accepting separation (October 8).** Stream bounded actual anatomy, ship bounds/corners, motion and camera geometry after presentation, linked to replay samples only during capture. Validate its completeness and projection independently. Preserve the first player's 215-row precision rejection and failed float-R repair; serialize the stored float through double round-trip formatting without widening tolerances. Distinguish conservative bounds from exact mesh collision and opened stills from continuous review. The valid capture confirms Sela/nose overlap and a slow ordinary movement escape, so the visual gate stays rejected while guidance diagnosis continues. [Evidence](validation/C4-drifter-fan.md).

- **D185 - Hold an outward escape until the departing body has passed (October 8).** Exact recorded poses distinguish proximity braking from a zero-waypoint deadlock inside intersected forecasts. Choose the nearest valid outward exit, preserve actual-body path constraints and wall priority, use ordinary thrust/boost, and delay regrouping through the unchanged bounded departure. Preserve every failed revision, including the 79/80 reverse-tail rejection after a smaller suite passed. Eighty affected checks and 22 rejecting controls support the bounded repair, not visual acceptance. The new ordinary run loses focus before Cantor and stays rejected with zero release frames. Label checkpoint messages as attempts, add read-only foreground identity telemetry, never force focus, and continue independent headless work while another application owns the foreground. Saves, frozen chapter and earlier C1/C2 failures remain protected. [Evidence](validation/C4-release-escape.md).

- **D186 - Stage the actual dormant animal and suspend automatic encounter entry explicitly (October 8).** Move the single Cantor closer to the existing viewing shoulder while keeping its complete anatomy inside activation and off the central approach. Preserve four substantial supported moorings; quiet their surface treatment without changing sockets or flight geometry. Retain the numerically passing but visually rejected surface revisions. Unity still sends triggers to disabled behaviours, so automatic entry checks isActiveAndEnabled; explicit Begin retains its semantics. Preserve older unisolated fixture results. Controlled background graphics help inspect real geometry while another application owns focus, but cannot accept ordinary or continuous quality. All unmet gates remain open. [Evidence](validation/C8-cantor-composition.md).

- **D187 — Make Tallow’s movement an observed, completion-owned discovery (October 9).** Preserve the actual refuge, free repair and saved slice completion; add an isolated port assembly with a slack free end and detached marker moving relative to the hull. Keeper’s acknowledgement is a separate completion-only flag, protected against cancellation, stale callbacks and changed owners. Preserve original failures and numerically passing visual rejections. Retain a smooth open line blockout after correcting crop/occlusion; no reeling animation or final-map acceptance is claimed. An extended ordinary route preserves every prior checkpoint field. Its executed failure and opened images now reject a travel budget and marker visibility behind dialogue; preserve them and repair the actual-duration scene next. All unmet quality gates remain open. [Evidence](validation/C8-tallow-mooring.md).

- **D188 - Frame Tallow through actual dialogue reading and preserve input failures (October 9).** Use a bounded scene camera lease with combat priority and explicit lifecycle return; reserve the actual overlay area without stopping or slowing the marker. Preserve the original crop/occlusion, startup-order failure, pause-fixture failure and null-device crash. Require graphics before rendered tests allocate their target. Extend the return route by insertion only, and report observed travel/focus separately from causal inference. Numerical clearance and opened stills do not accept continuous or final presentation. [Evidence](validation/C8-tallow-reading.md).

- **D189 - Every zone gets authored light and a sharp grade (November 3).** The maps had a pure-white sun, flat ambient and only bloom/vignette, so ground and interiors read flat. `ZoneLook.Apply` (isolated; never `WorldBuilder`) sets each zone's sun colour/intensity, a sky/equator/ground ambient gradient, distance fog for Sorrel and the yard, and a grade volume: contrast, saturation, restrained split toning outdoors, bloom tuned so emissive trim glows, and vignette. Sorrel's sun drops to 44° for longer ridge shadows; no other light moves. The first preview with Neutral tonemapping and strong split toning muddied the Decks and Tallow and was rejected after side-by-side review; tonemapping stays off. D060 holds: `ZoneLookTests` rejects any zone volume that blurs or warps (depth of field, motion blur, lens distortion, Panini, chromatic aberration), with an in-test negative control, and requires an authored grade (failed on the previous volumes). `LookDev.Render` is a read-only gameplay-angle still renderer for every zone (`-look-preview` applies the spec to temporary copies). The grade is a LUT plus fog/ambient, so its cost is expected to be negligible, but C1 timing has not been re-measured. Interiors still need layout and dressing work; colour alone does not fix empty, repeated floors. [Evidence](validation/C8-zone-look.md).

- **D190 - Publish a current workshop candidate beside the frozen chapter (November 3).** A month of repairs (Cantor collar/release, companion flight, Burrower shutdown, Tallow story, locomotion, lighting) had never reached a package Nathan could open; the frozen October 4 chapter (`a3aaad6`) still lacked them. Publish `Builds/Workshop-Nov03` from `a7409db` with its own profile, created by `tools/package_workshop.py` and launched with `workshop.ps1 -Package`, without touching the frozen package (all 243 files re-verified). Its New Game → Tallow chain passes by agent-directed virtual input. Presentation acceptance stays OPEN: the chain ran with displays asleep under explicit `-DiagnosticVSync 0`, because startup VSync reproduces D154's quarter-second stall (retained as `chain-01`), so it is progression evidence, not C1 timing. Publishing a candidate with explicit limits is a handoff for Nathan's own play, not C7 acceptance. [Evidence](validation/C7-nov03-chain.md), [player note](WORKSHOP_NOV03.md).
